([MidwestMotorBoy/Battery-Bank](https://github.com/MidwestMotorBoy/Battery-Bank/blob/HEAD/firmware/.claude/skills/ti-ccstudio-migrate-to-ti/agents/project-initializer-zephyr.md) · ★2)

- **project-manager.agent**: Project management specialist and TODO.md governor. Use proactively when: the user asks what to work on next, wants to plan a sprint or milestone, needs a feature broken down into tasks, asks about project progress or blockers, wants to reprioritize the backlog, or after a feature is completed and the backlog needs updating. Also invoke when multiple agents need to be coordinated for a larger piec ([hdang205/thuvienso](https://github.com/hdang205/thuvienso/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/project-manager.agent.md) · ★1)
- **project-manager.agent**: Orchestrate the full tsfagent workflow — take a user's project goal and drive it through specification writing, TSF quality statement authoring, and traceable implementation. Use when: starting a new project from a goal description, planning work across specification/TSF/implementation, coordinating the three tsfagent skills. Keywords: project, plan, orchestrate, workflow, goal, bootstrap, new project. ([AnotherDaniel/tsfagent](https://github.com/AnotherDaniel/tsfagent/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/project-manager.agent.md))
- **project-map**: MCP server with single `run` tool for LLM Python code execution. ([beycom/onetool-mcp](https://github.com/beycom/onetool-mcp/blob/HEAD/dev/agents/project-map.md) · ★24)
- **project-map**: Conceptual map of all 22 product domains: where the code lives, which schema module owns the data, which MCP tool family surfaces it, and the current shipping status. ([SimplerDevelopment/SimplerDevelopment](https://github.com/SimplerDevelopment/SimplerDevelopment/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/project-map.md) · ★18)
- **project-map**: Updates the project map: reads the code, git history and plans, and writes .project-map/map-data.js, which a ready-made page draws as parts, statuses, milestones, decisions and a suggested next step. Use before a long autonomous stretch, after a milestone, and whenever the map is out of date. It maps and does nothing else; it never changes project code. ([a-pavithraa/build-compass](https://github.com/a-pavithraa/build-compass/blob/HEAD/agents/project-map.md))
